	"id": "my-dashboard-hubs",

	"version": "0.1.27",
	"minAppVersion": "1.5.0",
	"description": "Нативный движок для главных страниц и хабов системы без перерисовки DataviewJS",
	"author": "Mersinho",
	"isDesktopOnly": false
}

```

## package.json
```json
{
	"name": "my-dashboard-hubs",
	"version": "0.1.27",
	"description": "Нативный движок для главных страниц и хабов системы",
	"main": "main.js",
	"scripts": {
		"dev": "node esbuild.config.mjs",
		"build": "tsc -noEmit -skipLibCheck && node esbuild.config.mjs production",
		"bump": "node version-bump.mjs",
		"import": "node import-scripts.mjs"
	},
	"keywords": [],
	"author": "Mersinho",
	"license": "MIT",
	"devDependencies": {
		"@types/node": "^16.11.6",
		"builtin-modules": "3.3.0",
		"esbuild": "0.17.3",
		"obsidian": "latest",
		"tslib": "2.4.0",
		"typescript": "4.7.4"
	}
}

```

## styles.css
```css
/* Единый файл стилей дашбордов (сгенерирован автоматически) */

.my-hub-native-container { overflow: visible !important; }

/* --- Module: home --- */
.dashboard-home .dh-today {
    background: var(--background-secondary);
    border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
    border-radius: 10px;
    padding: 16px 18px;
    margin: 0 0 20px 0;
    box-sizing: border-box;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today-head {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: flex-start;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 12px;
    padding-bottom: 12px;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today-titles {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 4px;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today .dh-kicker {
    font-size: 0.7rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.06em;
    color: var(--text-muted);
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today-date-row {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 10px;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today .dh-today-date {
    font-size: 1.2rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    color: var(--text-normal);
    line-height: 1.2;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today-badges {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 6px;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-status-pill {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 5px;
    padding: 2px 9px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    font-size: 0.74rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    line-height: 1.3;
    border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
    background: var(--background-primary);
    color: var(--text-muted);
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today-body {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 14px;
    margin-top: 14px;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today-section {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 10px;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today-row {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 112px 1fr;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 12px;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today-row-label {
    font-size: 0.82rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    color: var(--text-muted);
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: 6px;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today-row-sub {
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    color: var(--text-faint);
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today-chips {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 6px;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today .dh-score {
    min-width: 30px;
    height: 28px;
    padding: 0 6px;
    border-radius: 7px;
    font-size: 0.8rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    background: var(--background-primary);
    border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
    color: var(--text-muted);
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: border-color 0.14s ease, background-color 0.14s ease, color 0.14s ease, transform 0.1s ease;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today .dh-score:hover:not(:disabled) {
    border-color: var(--chip-color, var(--interactive-accent));
    color: var(--text-normal);
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today .dh-score.is-active {
    background: var(--chip-color, var(--interactive-accent));
    border-color: var(--chip-color, var(--interactive-accent));
    color: #141619;
    font-weight: 700;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today .dh-habit-chip {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 6px;
    height: 29px;
    padding: 0 11px;
    border-radius: 7px;
    font-size: 0.8rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    background: var(--background-primary);
    border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
    color: var(--text-muted);
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: border-color 0.14s ease, background-color 0.14s ease, color 0.14s ease;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today .dh-habit-chip:hover:not(:disabled) {
    border-color: var(--chip-color, var(--interactive-accent));
    color: var(--text-normal);
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today .dh-habit-chip .dh-habit-check {
    font-size: 0.74rem;
    line-height: 1;
    opacity: 0.65;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today .dh-habit-chip.is-active {
    background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--chip-color) 22%, var(--background-primary));
    border-color: var(--chip-color);
    color: var(--text-normal);
    font-weight: 600;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today .dh-habit-chip.is-active .dh-habit-check {
    color: var(--chip-color);
    opacity: 1;
    font-weight: 700;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-metrics-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(135px, 1fr));
    gap: 10px;
    padding-top: 12px;
    border-top: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-metric-item {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 4px;
    padding: 8px 10px;
    border-radius: 8px;
    background: var(--background-primary);
    border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
    transition: border-color 0.15s ease;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-metric-item.is-clickable {
    cursor: pointer;
    user-select: none;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-metric-item.is-clickable:hover {
    border-color: var(--interactive-accent);
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-metric-item.is-running {
    border-color: #9c83e8;
    background: color-mix(in srgb, #9c83e8 10%, var(--background-primary));
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-metric-item:focus-within {
    border-color: #599ed6;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-metric-item.is-invalid {
    border-color: #d85b57;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-metric-top {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 6px;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-metric-label {
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    color: var(--text-muted);
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-metric-status {
    font-size: 0.68rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    color: #4aab80;
    min-height: 1em;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-metric-control {
    display: flex;
    align-items: baseline;
    gap: 6px;
    min-height: 28px;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-metric-readonly-val {
    font-size: 1rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    color: var(--text-normal);
    line-height: 28px;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-metric-input {
    width: 78px !important;
    max-width: 100%;
    height: 28px !important;
    padding: 2px 8px !important;
    font-size: 0.88rem !important;
    font-weight: 600;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    text-align: right;
    border-radius: 6px !important;
    border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border) !important;
    background: var(--background-secondary) !important;
    color: var(--text-normal) !important;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-metric-input:focus {
    outline: none !important;
    border-color: #599ed6 !important;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-metric-unit {
    font-size: 0.78rem;
    color: var(--text-muted);
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-metric-subhint {
    font-size: 0.66rem;
    color: var(--text-faint);
    line-height: 1.2;
    overflow: hidden;
    text-overflow: ellipsis;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-metric-error {
    font-size: 0.7rem;
    color: #d85b57;
    line-height: 1.25;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today-bottom-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 10px;
    padding-top: 12px;
    border-top: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-plan-box {
    padding: 10px 12px;
    border-radius: 8px;
    background: var(--background-primary);
    border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 8px;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-plan-head {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-plan-title {
    font-size: 0.8rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    color: var(--text-muted);
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-plan-bar-track {
    height: 5px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    background: var(--background-modifier-border);
    overflow: hidden;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-plan-bar-fill {
    height: 100%;
    border-radius: 999px;
    transition: width 0.2s ease;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-plan-list {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(230px, 1fr));
    gap: 6px;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-plan-item {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: 8px;
    padding: 5px 8px;
    border-radius: 6px;
    background: var(--background-secondary);
    border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
    font-size: 0.8rem;
    cursor: pointer;
    user-select: none;
    transition: border-color 0.14s ease;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-plan-item:hover {
    border-color: #599ed688;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-plan-item.is-done {
    border-color: #4aab8055;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-plan-item-left {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 7px;
    min-width: 0;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-plan-item-right {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 6px;
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-plan-focus-btn {
    height: 22px !important;
    min-height: 22px !important;
    padding: 0 6px !important;
    border-radius: 5px !important;
    font-size: 0.68rem !important;
    font-weight: 600 !important;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    background: color-mix(in srgb, #9c83e8 14%, var(--background-primary)) !important;
    border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, #9c83e8 35%, var(--background-modifier-border)) !important;
    color: #9c83e8 !important;
    cursor: pointer;
    box-shadow: none !important;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-plan-focus-btn:hover {
    background: color-mix(in srgb, #9c83e8 24%, var(--background-primary)) !important;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-plan-check {
    width: 16px;
    height: 16px;
    border-radius: 4px;
    border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    font-size: 0.7rem;
    flex-shrink: 0;
    color: transparent;
    background: var(--background-primary);
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-plan-item.is-done .dh-plan-check {
    background: #4aab8022;
    border-color: #4aab80;
    color: #4aab80;
    font-weight: 700;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-plan-text {
    color: var(--text-normal);
    overflow: hidden;
    text-overflow: ellipsis;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-plan-item.is-done .dh-plan-text {
    color: var(--text-muted);
    text-decoration: line-through;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-plan-pct {
    font-size: 0.72rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    color: var(--text-muted);
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-event-row {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: 112px 1fr;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 12px;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-event-control {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px;
    width: 100%;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-event-input {
    flex: 1;
    width: 100% !important;
    height: 30px !important;
    padding: 4px 10px !important;
    font-size: 0.84rem !important;
    border-radius: 7px !important;
    border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border) !important;
    background: var(--background-primary) !important;
    color: var(--text-normal) !important;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-event-input:focus {
    outline: none !important;
    border-color: #599ed6 !important;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-today-footer {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 8px;
    padding-top: 10px;
    border-top: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
    font-size: 0.8rem;
    color: var(--text-muted);
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-footer-trainings {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    gap: 6px;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-training-pill {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 4px;
    padding: 2px 8px;
    border-radius: 6px;
    font-size: 0.76rem;
    font-weight: 500;
    background: var(--background-primary);
    border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
    color: var(--text-accent);
    text-decoration: none;
  }
  @media (max-width: 820px) {
    .dashboard-home .dh-metrics-grid {
      grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(120px, 1fr));
    }
  }
  @media (max-width: 560px) {
    .dashboard-home .dh-today-row,
    .dashboard-home .dh-event-row {
      grid-template-columns: 1fr;
      gap: 6px;
    }
    .dashboard-home .dh-metrics-grid {
      grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
    }
  }

/* --- Module: home --- */
.dashboard-home .dh-recent-work details.dh-recent-details > summary {
    list-style: none;
    display: flex;
    justify-content: space-between;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 10px;
    cursor: pointer;
    user-select: none;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-recent-work details.dh-recent-details > summary::-webkit-details-marker {
    display: none;
  }
  .dashboard-home .dh-recent-work details.dh-recent-details[open] > summary {
    margin-bottom: 14px;
    padding-bottom: 10px;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
  }

/* --- Module: diary --- */
.dh-calendar-v2 .dhc-panel {
        padding: 16px;
        border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
        border-radius: 12px;
        background: var(--background-secondary);
      }
      .dh-calendar-v2 .dhc-months {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(min(100%, 260px), 1fr));
        gap: 16px;
        align-items: start;
      }
      .dh-calendar-v2 .dhc-month {
        min-width: 0;
        padding: 12px;
        border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
        border-radius: 9px;
        background: var(--background-primary);
      }
      .dh-calendar-v2 .dhc-title {
        margin-bottom: 10px;
        padding-bottom: 8px;
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
        color: var(--text-normal);
        font-size: 0.88rem;
        font-weight: 600;
      }
      .dh-calendar-v2 .dhc-weekdays,
      .dh-calendar-v2 .dhc-days {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: repeat(7, minmax(0, 1fr));
        gap: 4px;
      }
      .dh-calendar-v2 .dhc-weekdays {
        margin-bottom: 6px;
        text-align: center;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        font-size: 0.7rem;
        font-weight: 600;
      }
      .dh-calendar-v2 .dhc-spacer {
        height: 32px;
      }
      .dh-calendar-v2 button.dhc-day {
        position: relative;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        width: 100%;
        min-width: 0;
        height: 32px;
        margin: 0;
        padding: 0;
        border: 1px solid transparent;
        border-radius: 5px;
        box-shadow: none;
        background: var(--background-secondary);
        color: var(--text-muted);
        font-family: inherit;
        font-size: 0.76rem;
        font-weight: 500;
        line-height: 1;
        cursor: pointer;
        transition: background-color 160ms ease, border-color 160ms ease, box-shadow 160ms ease;
      }
      .dh-calendar-v2 button.dhc-day.has-score {
        background: var(--day-background);
        border-color: var(--day-color);
        color: var(--text-normal);
      }
      .dh-calendar-v2 button.dhc-day.has-entry:not(.has-score) {
        border-style: dashed;
        border-color: var(--text-faint);
        background: var(--background-primary);
        color: var(--text-normal);
      }
      .dh-calendar-v2 button.dhc-day:hover:not(:disabled) {
        box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--text-muted);
      }
      .dh-calendar-v2 button.dhc-day.is-today {
        outline: 2px solid var(--interactive-accent);
        outline-offset: 1px;
        font-weight: 700;
      }
      .dh-calendar-v2 button.dhc-day:focus-visible {
        outline: 2px solid var(--text-normal);
        outline-offset: 2px;
      }
      .dh-calendar-v2 button.dhc-day.is-future {
        border-color: transparent;
        background: transparent;
        color: var(--text-faint);
        opacity: 0.38;
        cursor: default;
      }
      .dh-calendar-v2 .dhc-training {
        position: absolute;
        top: 2px;
        right: 2px;
        font-size: 0.56rem;
        line-height: 1;
        pointer-events: none;
      }
      .dh-calendar-v2 .dhc-legend {
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 7px;
        margin-top: 14px;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        font-size: 0.73rem;
      }
      .dh-calendar-v2 .dhc-swatch {
        display: inline-block;
        width: 12px;
        height: 12px;
        border-radius: 3px;
        flex: 0 0 auto;
      }
      .dh-calendar-v2 .dhc-no-score {
        margin-left: 6px;
        border: 1px dashed var(--text-faint);
        background: var(--background-primary);
      }
      .dhc-calendar-popover-v2 {
        position: fixed;
        z-index: 10000;
        box-sizing: border-box;
        width: 310px;
        max-width: calc(100vw - 20px);
        max-height: 70vh;
        overflow: auto;
        padding: 12px 14px;
        border: 1px solid var(--dhc-pop-accent, var(--background-modifier-border));
        border-radius: 9px;
        background: var(--background-primary);
        color: var(--text-normal);
        box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25);
        font-family: var(--font-interface);
        font-size: 0.8rem;
        line-height: 1.45;
        white-space: normal;
        overflow-wrap: anywhere;
        opacity: 0;
        visibility: hidden;
        pointer-events: none;
        transform: translateY(4px);
        transition: opacity 150ms ease, transform 150ms ease, visibility 150ms ease;
      }
      .dhc-calendar-popover-v2.is-open {
        opacity: 1;
        visibility: visible;
        pointer-events: auto;
        transform: translateY(0);
      }
      .dhc-calendar-popover-v2 .dhc-pop-date {
        margin-bottom: 8px;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        font-size: 0.74rem;
      }
      .dhc-calendar-popover-v2 .dhc-pop-score {
        display: flex;
        justify-content: space-between;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 12px;
      }
      .dhc-calendar-popover-v2 .dhc-pop-label {
        color: var(--text-muted);
      }
      .dhc-calendar-popover-v2 .dhc-pop-value {
        color: var(--dhc-pop-accent, var(--text-normal));
        font-weight: 650;
        text-align: right;
      }
      .dhc-calendar-popover-v2 .dhc-pop-event {
        margin-top: 10px;
        padding-top: 9px;
        border-top: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
      }
      .dhc-calendar-popover-v2 .dhc-pop-event-label {
        margin-bottom: 4px;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        font-size: 0.72rem;
      }
      .dhc-calendar-popover-v2 .dhc-pop-event-text {
        white-space: pre-wrap;
      }
      .dhc-calendar-popover-v2 .dhc-pop-empty {
        color: var(--text-muted);
      }
      .dhc-calendar-popover-v2 button.dhc-open-note {
        width: 100%;
        height: auto;
        margin: 11px 0 0;
        padding: 6px 9px;
        border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
        border-radius: 6px;
        box-shadow: none;
        background: var(--background-secondary);
        color: var(--text-normal);
        font-size: 0.75rem;
        cursor: pointer;
      }
      .dhc-calendar-popover-v2 button.dhc-open-note:hover {
        background: var(--background-modifier-hover);
      }
      @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
        .dh-calendar-v2 button.dhc-day,
        .dhc-calendar-popover-v2 {
          transition: none;
          transform: none;
        }
      }

/* --- Module: diary --- */
.dh-habits-v2 .dh-habit {
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        min-width: 0;
      }
      .dh-habits-v2 .dhhv-info {
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: space-between;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 6px 10px;
        min-height: 32px;
        margin: 8px 0;
        padding: 6px 8px;
        border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
        border-radius: 6px;
        background: var(--background-primary);
        font-size: 0.76rem;
      }
      .dh-habits-v2 .dhhv-footer {
        display: flex;
        justify-content: flex-start;
        margin-top: auto;
        padding-top: 9px;
      }
      .dh-habits-v2 button.dhhv-more {
        display: inline-flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        gap: 5px;
        min-height: 30px;
        height: auto;
        margin: 0;
        padding: 4px 9px;
        border: 1px solid transparent;
        border-radius: 6px;
        box-shadow: none;
        background: transparent;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        font-family: inherit;
        font-size: 0.73rem;
        line-height: 1.3;
        cursor: pointer;
        transition: color 160ms ease, background-color 160ms ease, border-color 160ms ease;
      }
      .dh-habits-v2 button.dhhv-more:hover,
      .dh-habits-v2 button.dhhv-more[aria-expanded="true"] {
        border-color: var(--background-modifier-border);
        background: var(--background-primary);
        color: var(--habit-color, var(--text-normal));
      }
      .dh-habits-v2 button.dhhv-more:focus-visible {
        outline: 2px solid var(--habit-color, var(--interactive-accent));
        outline-offset: 2px;
      }
      .dh-habits-v2 .dhhv-chevron {
        display: inline-block;
        font-size: 0.65rem;
        transition: transform 160ms ease;
      }
      .dh-habits-v2 button.dhhv-more[aria-expanded="true"] .dhhv-chevron {
        transform: rotate(180deg);
      }
      .dhhv-popover-v2 {
        position: fixed;
        z-index: 10000;
        box-sizing: border-box;
        width: 318px;
        max-width: calc(100vw - 20px);
        max-height: 75vh;
        overflow: auto;
        padding: 12px 14px;
        border: 1px solid var(--dhhv-accent);
        border-radius: 9px;
        background: var(--background-primary);
        color: var(--text-normal);
        box-shadow: 0 10px 28px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.28);
        font-family: var(--font-interface);
        font-size: 0.76rem;
        line-height: 1.38;
        overflow-wrap: anywhere;
        opacity: 0;
        visibility: hidden;
        pointer-events: none;
        transform: translateY(4px);
        transition: opacity 160ms ease, transform 160ms ease, visibility 160ms ease;
      }
      .dhhv-popover-v2.is-open {
        opacity: 1;
        visibility: visible;
        pointer-events: auto;
        transform: translateY(0);
      }
      .dhhv-popover-v2 .dhhv-pop-head {
        display: flex;
        justify-content: space-between;
        align-items: baseline;
        gap: 8px;
        margin-bottom: 8px;
        padding-bottom: 6px;
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
      }
      .dhhv-popover-v2 .dhhv-pop-title {
        color: var(--dhhv-accent);
        font-size: 0.83rem;
        font-weight: 650;
      }
      .dhhv-popover-v2 .dhhv-pop-period {
        color: var(--text-muted);
        font-size: 0.69rem;
        white-space: nowrap;
      }
      .dhhv-popover-v2 .dhhv-pop-section {
        margin-top: 8px;
        padding-top: 7px;
        border-top: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
      }
      .dhhv-popover-v2 .dhhv-pop-section:first-of-type {
        margin-top: 0;
        padding-top: 0;
        border-top: none;
      }
      .dhhv-popover-v2 .dhhv-pop-sec-title {
        margin-bottom: 4px;
        color: var(--text-muted);
        font-size: 0.68rem;
        font-weight: 650;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        letter-spacing: 0.04em;
      }
      .dhhv-popover-v2 .dhhv-pop-row {
        display: flex;
        justify-content: space-between;
        align-items: baseline;
        gap: 10px;
        padding: 3px 0;
      }
      .dhhv-popover-v2 .dhhv-pop-label {
        color: var(--text-muted);
      }
      .dhhv-popover-v2 .dhhv-pop-value {
        font-weight: 650;
        color: var(--text-normal);
        text-align: right;
        font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
      }
      .dhhv-popover-v2 .dhhv-pop-change-box {
        padding: 6px 8px;
        border-radius: 6px;
        background: var(--background-secondary);
        border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
        font-size: 0.74rem;
        line-height: 1.35;
      }
      .dhhv-popover-v2 .dhhv-pop-obs-list {
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 5px;
        margin: 0;
        padding: 0;
        list-style: none;
      }
      .dhhv-popover-v2 .dhhv-pop-obs-item {
        position: relative;
        padding-left: 11px;
        color: var(--text-normal);
        font-size: 0.74rem;
        line-height: 1.35;
      }
      .dhhv-popover-v2 .dhhv-pop-obs-item::before {
        content: "•";
        position: absolute;
        left: 0;
        top: 0;
        color: var(--dhhv-accent);
        font-weight: 700;
      }
      .dhhv-popover-v2 .dhhv-pop-muted {
        color: var(--text-muted);
        font-size: 0.73rem;
      }
      .dhhv-popover-v2 .dhhv-pop-secondary {
        display: flex;
        justify-content: space-between;
        align-items: center;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 6px;
        margin-top: 8px;
        padding-top: 7px;
        border-top: 1px dashed var(--background-modifier-border);
        color: var(--text-muted);
        font-size: 0.7rem;
      }

/* --- Module Array CSS: study --- */
.dashboard-home .dh-study-section { --dh-sp-s1: 4px; --dh-sp-s2: 8px; --dh-sp-s3: 12px; --dh-sp-s4: 16px; --dh-sp-fs-title: 1.02rem; --dh-sp-fs: 0.84rem; --dh-sp-fs-xs: 0.74rem; box-sizing: border-box; width: 100%; }
.dashboard-home .dh-study-section * { box-sizing: border-box; }
.dashboard-home .dh-study-section button, .dashboard-home .dh-study-section select { touch-action: manipulation; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-topbar { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: var(--dh-sp-s2) var(--dh-sp-s3); margin-bottom: var(--dh-sp-s3); }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-topbar-left { display: flex; align-items: baseline; gap: var(--dh-sp-s2); flex-wrap: wrap; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-heading { font-size: var(--dh-sp-fs-title); font-weight: 600; color: var(--text-normal); }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-summary { font-size: var(--dh-sp-fs-xs); color: var(--text-muted); }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-topbar-actions { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--dh-sp-s2); flex-wrap: wrap; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-btn { height: 32px !important; min-height: 32px !important; padding: 0 var(--dh-sp-s3) !important; border-radius: 8px !important; font-size: var(--dh-sp-fs) !important; font-weight: 500 !important; background: var(--background-secondary) !important; border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border) !important; color: var(--text-normal) !important; cursor: pointer; box-shadow: none !important; display: inline-flex !important; align-items: center !important; justify-content: center !important; white-space: nowrap; transition: background-color 0.16s ease, border-color 0.16s ease, color 0.16s ease, transform 0.12s ease; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-btn:hover { background: var(--background-modifier-hover) !important; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-btn:active { transform: scale(0.97); }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-btn.is-accent { color: var(--interactive-accent) !important; background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--interactive-accent) 12%, var(--background-secondary)) !important; border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--interactive-accent) 36%, var(--background-modifier-border)) !important; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-btn.is-accent:hover { background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--interactive-accent) 20%, var(--background-secondary)) !important; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-btn.is-active { border-color: var(--interactive-accent) !important; color: var(--interactive-accent) !important; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-new-box { padding: var(--dh-sp-s4); margin-bottom: var(--dh-sp-s4); background: var(--background-secondary); border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border); border-radius: 12px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--dh-sp-s2); animation: dh-sp-fade-in 0.18s ease-out; }
@keyframes dh-sp-fade-in { from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(-4px); } to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); } }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-new-row { display: flex; gap: var(--dh-sp-s2); align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap; min-width: 0; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-icon-pick-btn { width: 32px !important; min-width: 32px !important; height: 32px !important; min-height: 32px !important; padding: 0 !important; border-radius: 8px !important; font-size: var(--dh-sp-fs-title) !important; display: inline-flex !important; align-items: center !important; justify-content: center !important; background: var(--background-primary) !important; border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border) !important; cursor: pointer; box-shadow: none !important; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-input { height: 32px; min-height: 32px; padding: 0 var(--dh-sp-s2); border-radius: 8px; font-size: var(--dh-sp-fs); min-width: 0; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-icons-bar { display: flex; gap: var(--dh-sp-s1); align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap; padding-top: var(--dh-sp-s2); border-top: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border); }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-grid { display: grid; width: 100%; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(min(100%, 300px), 1fr)); gap: var(--dh-sp-s4); margin-bottom: var(--dh-sp-s4); align-items: stretch; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-grid[data-count='1'] { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 520px); }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-card { position: relative; width: 100%; min-width: 0; overflow: hidden; padding: var(--dh-sp-s4) !important; background: var(--background-secondary); border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border); border-top: 3px solid var(--card-accent, var(--interactive-accent)); border-radius: 12px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; justify-content: space-between; gap: var(--dh-sp-s3); transition: border-color 0.16s ease, background-color 0.16s ease; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-card:hover { border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--card-accent, var(--interactive-accent)) 50%, var(--background-modifier-border)); border-top-color: var(--card-accent, var(--interactive-accent)); }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-card-top { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--dh-sp-s2); min-width: 0; width: 100%; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-card-head { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: flex-start; gap: var(--dh-sp-s2); min-width: 0; width: 100%; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-card-badges { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--dh-sp-s2); min-width: 0; flex: 1 1 auto; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-type-pill { font-size: var(--dh-sp-fs-xs); font-weight: 600; padding: 2px var(--dh-sp-s2); border-radius: 6px; background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--card-accent, #599ed6) 15%, transparent); color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--card-accent, #599ed6) 90%, var(--text-normal)); white-space: nowrap; flex-shrink: 0; line-height: 1.4; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-discipline { font-size: var(--dh-sp-fs-xs); color: var(--text-muted); min-width: 0; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-deadline { height: 24px !important; min-height: 24px !important; padding: 0 var(--dh-sp-s2) !important; border-radius: 6px !important; font-size: var(--dh-sp-fs-xs) !important; font-weight: 500 !important; border: 1px solid transparent !important; box-shadow: none !important; cursor: pointer; white-space: nowrap; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; flex-shrink: 0; display: inline-flex !important; align-items: center !important; justify-content: center !important; transition: background-color 0.15s ease, border-color 0.15s ease; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-deadline.is-far { color: var(--color-green, #4aab80) !important; background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-green, #4aab80) 13%, transparent) !important; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-deadline.is-soon { color: var(--color-orange, #e5a158) !important; background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-orange, #e5a158) 15%, transparent) !important; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-deadline.is-overdue { color: var(--text-error, #e06c75) !important; background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--text-error, #e06c75) 15%, transparent) !important; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-deadline.is-empty { color: var(--text-muted) !important; background: var(--background-primary) !important; border-color: var(--background-modifier-border) !important; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-deadline:hover { border-color: color-mix(in srgb, currentColor 40%, transparent) !important; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-title-link { font-size: var(--dh-sp-fs-title); font-weight: 600; color: var(--text-normal); text-decoration: none; line-height: 1.38; overflow-wrap: anywhere; word-break: break-word; margin: 2px 0; display: block; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-title-link:hover { color: var(--text-accent); }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-card-bottom { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--dh-sp-s3); min-width: 0; width: 100%; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-meta-row { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; gap: var(--dh-sp-s2); min-width: 0; width: 100%; padding-top: var(--dh-sp-s2); border-top: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border); }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-meta-text { font-size: var(--dh-sp-fs-xs); color: var(--text-muted); font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; min-width: 0; flex: 1 1 auto; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-card-tools { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--dh-sp-s1); flex-shrink: 0; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-icon-action { width: 28px !important; min-width: 28px !important; height: 28px !important; min-height: 28px !important; padding: 0 !important; border-radius: 6px !important; font-size: 0.78rem !important; font-weight: 500 !important; line-height: 1 !important; color: var(--text-muted) !important; background: transparent !important; border: 1px solid transparent !important; box-shadow: none !important; cursor: pointer; display: inline-flex !important; align-items: center !important; justify-content: center !important; flex-shrink: 0; opacity: 0.55; transition: opacity 0.15s ease, background-color 0.15s ease, border-color 0.15s ease, color 0.15s ease, transform 0.12s ease; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-card:hover .dh-sp-icon-action, .dashboard-home .dh-sp-card:focus-within .dh-sp-icon-action, .dashboard-home .dh-sp-icon-action.is-active { opacity: 1; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-icon-action:hover, .dashboard-home .dh-sp-icon-action.is-active { background: var(--background-primary) !important; border-color: var(--background-modifier-border) !important; color: var(--text-normal) !important; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-icon-action:active { transform: scale(0.94); }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-icon-action.is-done:hover { color: var(--color-green, #4aab80) !important; border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-green, #4aab80) 40%, var(--background-modifier-border)) !important; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-icon-action.is-danger { width: auto !important; padding: 0 var(--dh-sp-s2) !important; opacity: 1 !important; color: var(--text-error, #e06c75) !important; background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--text-error, #e06c75) 14%, var(--background-primary)) !important; border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--text-error, #e06c75) 45%, var(--background-modifier-border)) !important; font-size: var(--dh-sp-fs-xs) !important; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-date-popover { padding: var(--dh-sp-s2); background: var(--background-primary); border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border); display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: var(--dh-sp-s1); align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; width: 100%; min-width: 0; animation: dh-sp-fade-in 0.16s ease-out; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-card-footer { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--dh-sp-s2); width: 100%; min-width: 0; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-open-btn { flex: 1 1 0%; min-width: 0 !important; height: 32px !important; min-height: 32px !important; padding: 0 var(--dh-sp-s3) !important; border-radius: 8px !important; font-size: var(--dh-sp-fs) !important; font-weight: 500 !important; color: var(--interactive-accent) !important; background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--interactive-accent) 12%, var(--background-primary)) !important; border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--interactive-accent) 32%, var(--background-modifier-border)) !important; box-shadow: none !important; cursor: pointer; display: inline-flex !important; align-items: center !important; justify-content: center !important; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; transition: background-color 0.15s ease, border-color 0.15s ease, transform 0.12s ease; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-open-btn:hover { background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--interactive-accent) 20%, var(--background-primary)) !important; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-open-btn:active { transform: scale(0.98); }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-plus-btn { flex: 0 0 32px !important; width: 32px !important; min-width: 32px !important; height: 32px !important; min-height: 32px !important; padding: 0 !important; border-radius: 8px !important; font-size: 1rem !important; font-weight: 500 !important; line-height: 1 !important; color: var(--text-normal) !important; background: var(--background-primary) !important; border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border) !important; box-shadow: none !important; cursor: pointer; display: inline-flex !important; align-items: center !important; justify-content: center !important; transition: color 0.15s ease, border-color 0.15s ease, background-color 0.15s ease, transform 0.12s ease; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-plus-btn:hover { color: var(--interactive-accent) !important; border-color: var(--interactive-accent) !important; background: var(--background-modifier-hover) !important; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-plus-btn:active { transform: scale(0.94); }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-orphans { padding: var(--dh-sp-s4); margin-bottom: var(--dh-sp-s4); background: var(--background-secondary); border-radius: 12px; border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border); display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--dh-sp-s2); }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-orphan-row { display: flex; gap: var(--dh-sp-s2); align-items: center; padding: var(--dh-sp-s1) 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border); flex-wrap: wrap; }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-done-details { padding: var(--dh-sp-s3) var(--dh-sp-s4); background: var(--background-secondary); border-radius: 12px; border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border); }
.dashboard-home .dh-sp-done-row { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: var(--dh-sp-s2); padding: var(--dh-sp-s2) var(--dh-sp-s3); background: var(--background-primary); border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border); }
.dashboard-home .dh-study-cards-hub { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 14px; margin: 0 0 18px 0; box-sizing: border-box; }
.dashboard-home .dh-study-cards-hub .dsc-panel { padding: 14px 16px; background: var(--background-secondary); border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border); border-radius: 10px; box-sizing: border-box; }
.dashboard-home .dh-study-cards-hub .dsc-head { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 10px; margin-bottom: 12px; }
.dashboard-home .dh-study-cards-hub .dsc-title { font-size: 1rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--text-normal); display: inline-flex; align-items: baseline; gap: 8px; }
.dashboard-home .dh-study-cards-hub .dsc-decks-bar { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-bottom: 12px; padding-bottom: 10px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border); }
.dashboard-home .dh-study-cards-hub .dsc-deck-chip { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; padding: 4px 11px; height: 28px; border-radius: 999px; font-size: 0.77rem; font-weight: 600; background: var(--background-primary); border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border); color: var(--text-muted); cursor: pointer; transition: all 0.14s ease; }
.dashboard-home .dh-study-cards-hub .dsc-deck-chip:hover { border-color: #9c83e8; color: var(--text-normal); }
.dashboard-home .dh-study-cards-hub .dsc-deck-chip.is-active { background: color-mix(in srgb, #9c83e8 18%, var(--background-primary)); border-color: #9c83e8; color: var(--text-normal); }

## src/main.ts
```ts
import {
  App,
  MarkdownPostProcessorContext,
  MarkdownRenderChild,
  Notice,
  Plugin,
  PluginSettingTab,
  Setting,
  TFile,
} from "obsidian";
import {
  buildDvShim,
  parseHubCodeblockSource,
  runBundledHubModule,
} from "./shim";
import { BUNDLED_HUB_SCRIPTS } from "./modules";

interface HubPluginSettings {
  scriptsFolder: string;
  autoRefreshOnMetadataChange: boolean;
  refreshDebounceMs: number;
}

const DEFAULT_SETTINGS: HubPluginSettings = {
  scriptsFolder: "Скрипты",
  autoRefreshOnMetadataChange: true,
  refreshDebounceMs: 600,
};

const MODULE_FILE_CANDIDATES: Record<string, string[]> = {
  home: ["home.js", "главная.js", "Scripts/home.js"],
  diary: ["diary.js", "дневник.js", "Scripts/diary.js"],
  health: ["health.js", "здоровье.js", "Scripts/health.js"],
  study: ["study.js", "учеба.js", "Scripts/study.js"],
  "3d": ["3d.js", "3D.js", "Scripts/3d.js"],
  english: ["english2.js", "english.js", "английский.js", "Scripts/english.js"],
  media: ["media.js", "медиа.js", "Scripts/media.js"], // <-- ДОБАВИТЬ
};

class HubBlockRenderer extends MarkdownRenderChild {
  plugin: MyDashboardHubsPlugin;
  source: string;
  ctx: MarkdownPostProcessorContext;
  private refreshTimer: number | null = null;
  private isRendering = false;
  private pendingRerender = false;

  constructor(
    containerEl: HTMLElement,
    plugin: MyDashboardHubsPlugin,
    source: string,
    ctx: MarkdownPostProcessorContext
  ) {
    super(containerEl);
    this.plugin = plugin;
    this.source = source;
    this.ctx = ctx;
  }

  onload() {
    this.plugin.activeRenderers.add(this);
    void this.renderBlock();

    if (this.plugin.settings.autoRefreshOnMetadataChange) {
      this.registerEvent(
        this.plugin.app.metadataCache.on("changed", (file) => {
          this.scheduleSmartRefresh(file.path);
        })
      );
    }
  }

  onunload() {
    this.plugin.activeRenderers.delete(this);
    if (this.refreshTimer !== null) {
      window.clearTimeout(this.refreshTimer);
      this.refreshTimer = null;
    }
  }

  scheduleSmartRefresh(_changedPath?: string) {
    if (this.refreshTimer !== null) {
      window.clearTimeout(this.refreshTimer);
    }
    this.refreshTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      this.refreshTimer = null;
      if (!this.containerEl.isConnected) return;

      const activeEl = document.activeElement;
      const isFocusedInside =
        activeEl &&
        (this.containerEl.contains(activeEl) ||
          activeEl.tagName === "INPUT" ||
          activeEl.tagName === "TEXTAREA" ||
          activeEl.tagName === "SELECT");

      if (isFocusedInside || document.querySelector(".modal-container")) {
        return;
      }

      void this.renderBlock();
    }, this.plugin.settings.refreshDebounceMs);
  }

  async renderBlock() {
    if (this.isRendering) {
      this.pendingRerender = true;
      return;
    }
    this.isRendering = true;

    try {
      const params = parseHubCodeblockSource(this.source);
      const codeBody = await this.plugin.resolveModuleScriptCode(params.module);

      if (!codeBody) {
        this.containerEl.empty();
        const err = this.containerEl.createDiv({ cls: "dh-panel" });
        err.setText(
          `Модуль «${params.module}» не найден. Запусти "npm run import" при сборке или проверь папку скриптов в настройках.`
        );
        return;
      }

      const prevHeight = this.containerEl.offsetHeight;
      if (prevHeight > 40) {
        this.containerEl.style.minHeight = `${prevHeight}px`;
      }

      this.containerEl.empty();
      this.containerEl.addClass("dashboard-home", "my-hub-native-container");

      const dvShim = buildDvShim(
        this.plugin.app,
        this.containerEl,
        this.ctx,
        this
      );
      await runBundledHubModule(codeBody, this.plugin.app, dvShim, {
        section: params.section,
      });

      this.containerEl.style.minHeight = "";
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      this.containerEl.style.minHeight = "";
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.stack || err.message : String(err);
      this.containerEl.createDiv({
        text: `Ошибка модуля дашборда: ${msg}`,
        attr: {
          style:
            "padding: 12px; color: #d85b57; background: rgba(216,91,87,0.12); border: 1px solid #d85b57; border-radius: 8px; white-space: pre-wrap; font-family: monospace; font-size: 0.8rem;",
        },
      });
    } finally {
      this.isRendering = false;
      if (this.pendingRerender) {
        this.pendingRerender = false;
        void this.renderBlock();
      }
    }
  }
}

class HubsSettingTab extends PluginSettingTab {
  plugin: MyDashboardHubsPlugin;

  constructor(app: App, plugin: MyDashboardHubsPlugin) {
    super(app, plugin);
    this.plugin = plugin;
  }

  display(): void {
    const { containerEl } = this;
    containerEl.empty();

    containerEl.createEl("h3", { text: "Мои дашборды (Ускоритель)" });

    new Setting(containerEl)
      .setName("Папка со скриптами в хранилище (резервная)")
      .setDesc(
        "Если скрипт не вшит внутрь плагина, плагин может подгружать .js файл напрямую из этой папки хранилища"
      )
      .addText((text) =>
        text
          .setPlaceholder("Скрипты")
          .setValue(this.plugin.settings.scriptsFolder)
          .onChange(async (val) => {
            this.plugin.settings.scriptsFolder = val.trim();
            await this.plugin.saveSettings();
          })
      );

    new Setting(containerEl)
      .setName("Автообновление при изменении заметок")
      .setDesc(
        "Автоматически обновлять карточки и счётчики в фоне (без сброса фокуса в полях ввода)"
      )
      .addToggle((toggle) =>
        toggle
          .setValue(this.plugin.settings.autoRefreshOnMetadataChange)
          .onChange(async (val) => {
            this.plugin.settings.autoRefreshOnMetadataChange = val;
            await this.plugin.saveSettings();
          })
      );
  }
}

export default class MyDashboardHubsPlugin extends Plugin {
  settings!: HubPluginSettings;
  activeRenderers = new Set<HubBlockRenderer>();

  async onload() {
    this.settings = Object.assign({}, DEFAULT_SETTINGS, await this.loadData());
    this.addSettingTab(new HubsSettingTab(this.app, this));

    this.registerMarkdownCodeBlockProcessor("my-hub", (source, el, ctx) => {
      const child = new HubBlockRenderer(el, this, source, ctx);
      ctx.addChild(child);
    });

    const shortModules = ["home", "diary", "health", "study", "3d", "english", "media"]; // <-- ДОБАВИТЬ "media"
    for (const modName of shortModules) {
      this.registerMarkdownCodeBlockProcessor(
        `my-${modName}`,
        (source, el, ctx) => {
          const trimmed = source.trim();
          const fullSource = trimmed
            ? `module: ${modName}\nsection: ${trimmed}`
            : `module: ${modName}`;
          const child = new HubBlockRenderer(el, this, fullSource, ctx);
          ctx.addChild(child);
        }
      );
    }

    this.addCommand({
      id: "refresh-active-hubs",
      name: "Обновить все открытые дашборды",
      callback: () => {
        for (const r of this.activeRenderers) {
          void r.renderBlock();
        }
        new Notice("Дашборды обновлены");
      },
    });
  }

  async resolveModuleScriptCode(moduleName: string): Promise<string | null> {
    const key = moduleName.toLowerCase().trim();

    const bundled = BUNDLED_HUB_SCRIPTS[key];
    if (bundled && bundled.length > 10) {
      return bundled;
    }

    const folder = this.settings.scriptsFolder.replace(/\/+$/, "");
    const candidates = [
      ...(folder ? [`${folder}/${key}.js`] : []),
      ...(MODULE_FILE_CANDIDATES[key] ?? [`${key}.js`]),
    ];

    for (const candidatePath of candidates) {
      const file = this.app.vault.getAbstractFileByPath(candidatePath);
      if (file instanceof TFile) {
        try {
          return await this.app.vault.cachedRead(file);
        } catch {
          // Игнорируем
        }
      }
    }

    return null;
  }

  async saveSettings() {
    await this.saveData(this.settings);
  }
}
```

## src/modules.ts
```ts
// АВТОМАТИЧЕСКИ СГЕНЕРИРОВАННЫЙ ФАЙЛ (npm run import)
export const BUNDLED_HUB_SCRIPTS: Record<string, string> = {
  "home": "// ========================================================\n// ОБЩИЕ ДАННЫЕ, АВТОСИНХРОНИЗАЦИЯ И УТИЛИТЫ (ГЛАВНАЯ v2.3)\n// ========================================================\nconst AUTO_SLEEP_MIN_HOURS = 6;   // Сон > 6 часов автоматически отмечает «Режим сна»\nconst SOCIAL_LIMIT_MIN = 30;       // Лимит дофаминового скроллинга соцсетей (мин)\nconst BOOKS_FOLDER = \"Знания/Книги\";\nconst READING_TRACKER_KEY = \"dh_reading_daily_tracker_v1\";\nconst MANUAL_HABITS_PREFIX = \"dh_today_manual_habits_v1:\";\n\nconst empty = v => v == null || v === \"\";\nfunction asDate(v) {\n  if (empty(v)) return null;\n  try { const d = dv.date(v); return d && d.isValid ? d.startOf(\"day\") : null; } catch { return null; }\n}\nconst mtime = p => { try { return p.file.mtime?.toMillis?.() ?? 0; } catch { return 0; } };\nconst notify = message => { try { new Notice(message); } catch { console.log(message); } };\n\nfunction parseNum(v) {\n  if (typeof v === \"number\") return Number.isFinite(v) ? v : null;\n  if (typeof v !== \"string\") return null;\n  const s = v.trim().replace(\",\", \".\");\n  if (s === \"\") return null;\n  return /^[+-]?(?:\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?|\\.\\d+)$/.test(s) ? Number(s) : null;\n}\n\nfunction getNutritionPlugin() {\n  return app.plugins?.plugins?.[\"my-nutrition-tracker\"] ?? null;\n}\n\nfunction getWorkoutPlugin() {\n  return app.plugins?.plugins?.[\"my-workout-companion\"] ?? null;\n}\n\nfunction getFocusPlugin() {\n  return app.plugins?.plugins?.[\"my-focus-tracker\"] || window.__myFocusTracker || null;\n}\n\nfunction openFocusTrackerFromHome(initialProject) {\n  const plugin = getFocusPlugin();\n  if (!plugin || typeof plugin.openFocusModal !== \"function\") {\n    notify(\"Включи плагин «Фокус-таймер» (my-focus-tracker) в настройках Obsidian.\");\n    return;\n  }\n  try {\n    plugin.openFocusModal(initialProject ? { initialProject } : {});\n  } catch (err) {\n    notify(\"Не удалось открыть таймер фокуса: \" + (err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)));\n  }\n}\n\n// Читаем дневную цель калорий напрямую из настроек my-nutrition-tracker (если цель отключена — null)\nconst nutPlugin = getNutritionPlugin();\nconst rawCalorieGoal = nutPlugin?.settings ? nutPlugin.settings.dailyCalorieGoal : null;\nconst calorieGoal = typeof rawCalorieGoal === \"number\" && rawCalorieGoal > 0 ? Math.round(rawCalorieGoal) : null;\n\nconst today = dv.date(\"today\").startOf(\"day\");\nconst todayISO = today.toISODate();\n\nconst todayNote = dv.pages('\"Ежедневник\"').array()\n  .filter(p => (asDate(p.дата) || asDate(p.file.name))?.toISODate() === todayISO)\n  .sort((a, b) => mtime(b) - mtime(a) || a.file.path.localeCompare(b.file.path, \"ru\"))[0] || null;\n\nconst knowledgePages = dv.pages('\"Знания\"').array();\nconst trainingsTodayList = knowledgePages.filter(\n  p => p.тип === \"Тренировка\" && asDate(p.дата)?.toISODate() === todayISO\n);\n\n// --- Чтение/запись ручных переопределений привычек за сегодня ---\nfunction getManualHabitOverrides() {\n  try {\n    const raw = window.localStorage?.getItem(MANUAL_HABITS_PREFIX + todayISO);\n    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};\n  } catch {\n    return {};\n  }\n}\nfunction setManualHabitOverride(key, val) {\n  try {\n    const cur = getManualHabitOverrides();\n    if (val === undefined) delete cur[key];\n    else cur[key] = val;\n    window.localStorage?.setItem(MANUAL_HABITS_PREFIX + todayISO, JSON.stringify(cur));\n  } catch {}\n}\n\n// --- Автоматический подсчёт прочитанных за сегодня страниц из заметок книг ---\nfunction computeTodayReadingProgress() {\n  let bookPages = [];\n  try {\n    bookPages = dv.pages('\"' + BOOKS_FOLDER + '\"').array();\n  } catch {\n    bookPages = [];\n  }\n\n  let tracker = null;\n  try {\n    const raw = window.localStorage?.getItem(READING_TRACKER_KEY);\n    if (raw) tracker = JSON.parse(raw);\n  } catch {}\n\n  if (!tracker || typeof tracker !== \"object\") {\n    tracker = { dateISO: todayISO, baselines: {}, lastKnown: {}, manualOffset: 0 };\n  }\n  if (!tracker.baselines || typeof tracker.baselines !== \"object\") tracker.baselines = {};\n  if (!tracker.lastKnown || typeof tracker.lastKnown !== \"object\") tracker.lastKnown = {};\n  if (typeof tracker.manualOffset !== \"number\" || !Number.isFinite(tracker.manualOffset)) tracker.manualOffset = 0;\n\n  const isNewDay = tracker.dateISO !== todayISO;\n  if (isNewDay) {\n    const nextBaselines = {};\n    for (const p of bookPages) {\n      const f = app.vault.getAbstractFileByPath(p.file.path);\n      const fm = f ? (app.metadataCache.getFileCache(f)?.frontmatter || {}) : {};\n      const curRead = Math.max(0, Math.round(parseNum(fm.прочитано ?? p.прочитано) || 0));\n      const prevKnown = tracker.lastKnown[p.file.path];\n      nextBaselines[p.file.path] = (typeof prevKnown === \"number\" && Number.isFinite(prevKnown))\n        ? Math.min(prevKnown, curRead)\n        : curRead;\n      tracker.lastKnown[p.file.path] = curRead;\n    }\n    tracker.dateISO = todayISO;\n    tracker.baselines = nextBaselines;\n    tracker.manualOffset = 0;\n  }\n\n  let autoBookPages = 0;\n  let readBookTitles = [];\n\n  for (const p of bookPages) {\n    const f = app.vault.getAbstractFileByPath(p.file.path);\n    const fm = f ? (app.metadataCache.getFileCache(f)?.frontmatter || {}) : {};\n    const curRead = Math.max(0, Math.round(parseNum(fm.прочитано ?? p.прочитано) || 0));\n    const title = String(fm.название ?? p.название ?? p.file.name).trim();\n\n    const lastReadRaw = fm.последнее_чтение ?? p.последнее_чтение;\n    const lastReadISO = asDate(lastReadRaw)?.toISODate() || \"\";\n    const posTimeRaw = String(fm.позиция_время ?? p.позиция_время ?? \"\").trim();\n    const posDateISO = posTimeRaw.length >= 10 ? posTimeRaw.slice(0, 10) : \"\";\n    const touchedToday = lastReadISO === todayISO || posDateISO === todayISO;\n\n    if (tracker.baselines[p.file.path] === undefined) {\n      tracker.baselines[p.file.path] = curRead;\n    } else if (curRead < tracker.baselines[p.file.path]) {\n      tracker.baselines[p.file.path] = curRead;\n    }\n\n    const delta = Math.max(0, curRead - tracker.baselines[p.file.path]);\n    if (delta > 0) {\n      autoBookPages += delta;\n      readBookTitles.push(`${title} (+${delta})`);\n    } else if (touchedToday && !readBookTitles.includes(title)) {\n      readBookTitles.push(title);\n    }\n\n    tracker.lastKnown[p.file.path] = curRead;\n  }\n\n  try {\n    window.localStorage?.setItem(READING_TRACKER_KEY, JSON.stringify(tracker));\n  } catch {}\n\n  const totalPages = Math.max(0, autoBookPages + tracker.manualOffset);\n  return {\n    autoBookPages,\n    manualOffset: tracker.manualOffset,\n    totalPages,\n    readBookTitles\n  };\n}\n\nfunction updateReadingManualOffset(newTotalPages, autoBookPages) {\n  try {\n    const raw = window.localStorage?.getItem(READING_TRACKER_KEY);\n    const tracker = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : { dateISO: todayISO, baselines: {}, lastKnown: {}, manualOffset: 0 };\n    tracker.dateISO = todayISO;\n    tracker.manualOffset = Math.round((Number(newTotalPages) || 0) - (Number(autoBookPages) || 0));\n    window.localStorage?.setItem(READING_TRACKER_KEY, JSON.stringify(tracker));\n  } catch {}\n}\n\nconst readingSync = computeTodayReadingProgress();\n\n// ========================================================\n// 1. НАВИГАЦИОННЫЕ ПЛИТКИ (ХАБЫ СИСТЕМЫ)\n// ========================================================\n{\n  const isTemplate = p => p.file.name.startsWith(\"Учёба — \") || p.file.path.startsWith(\"Шаблоны/\");\n  const allPages = dv.pages().array();\n\n  const studyActive = allPages.filter(p =>\n    !isTemplate(p) &&\n    String(p.направление ?? \"\").trim() === \"Учёба\" &&\n    [\"Проект\", \"Лабораторная работа\"].includes(String(p.тип ?? \"\").trim()) &&\n    ![\"завершён\", \"завершена\", \"сдана\", \"сдан\", \"готово\", \"архив\", \"отменён\", \"отменена\"].includes(String(p.статус ?? \"\").trim().toLowerCase())\n  ).length;\n\n  const trainingsToday = trainingsTodayList.length;\n\n  const notes3D = allPages.filter(p =>\n    !isTemplate(p) &&\n    String(p.направление ?? \"\").trim() === \"3D\" &&\n    p.file.name !== \"🧊3D\"\n  ).length;\n\n  const mediaCount = allPages.filter(p =>\n    !isTemplate(p) &&\n    p.file.path.startsWith(\"Знания/Медиа/\") &&\n    p.file.name !== \"🎬Медиатека\"\n  ).length;\n\n  const cardsDoneToday = parseNum(todayNote?.карточек_пройдено) || 0;\n  const pagesDoneToday = Math.max(parseNum(todayNote?.страниц_прочитано) || 0, readingSync.totalPages);\n  let englishBadge = \"Карточки и книги\";\n  if (cardsDoneToday > 0 && pagesDoneToday > 0) {\n    englishBadge = `${cardsDoneToday} карт. · ${pagesDoneToday} стр.`;\n  } else if (cardsDoneToday > 0) {\n    englishBadge = `Сегодня: ${cardsDoneToday} карт.`;\n  } else if (pagesDoneToday > 0) {\n    englishBadge = `Чтение: ${pagesDoneToday} стр.`;\n  }\n\n  const HUBS = [\n    {\n      title: \"Дневник\",\n      file: \"📓Дневник\",\n      icon: \"📓\",\n      color: \"#9c83e8\",\n      badge: todayNote ? (todayNote.оценка_дня ? `Оценка: ${todayNote.оценка_дня}/10` : \"Запись открыта\") : \"Нет записи\"\n    },\n    {\n      title: \"Учёба\",\n      file: \"🎓Учёба\",\n      icon: \"🎓\",\n      color: \"#e5a158\",\n      badge: `В работе: ${studyActive}`\n    },\n    {\n      title: \"Здоровье\",\n      file: \"💪Здоровье\",\n      icon: \"💪\",\n      color: \"#d69852\",\n      badge: trainingsToday > 0 || todayNote?.тренировка === true ? \"Тренировка ✓\" : \"Тренировки и вес\"\n    },\n    {\n      title: \"Английский\",\n      file: \"🌎Английский и книги\",\n      icon: \"🌎\",\n      color: \"#4aab80\",\n      badge: englishBadge\n    },\n    {\n      title: \"3D\",\n      file: \"🧊3D\",\n      icon: \"🧊\",\n      color: \"#599ed6\",\n      badge: `Заметок: ${notes3D}`\n    },\n    {\n      title: \"Медиатека\",\n      file: \"Знания/Медиа/🎬Медиатека\",\n      icon: \"🎬\",\n      color: \"#e06c75\",\n      badge: `${mediaCount} позиций`\n    }\n  ];\n\n  const root = dv.container.createDiv({ cls: \"dh dh-hub-nav\", attr: { style: \"margin: 6px 0 22px 0;\" } });\n\n  const grid = root.createDiv({\n    attr: {\n      style: \"display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr)); gap: 12px;\"\n    }\n  });\n\n  HUBS.forEach(hub => {\n    const card = grid.createDiv({\n      cls: \"dh-card\",\n      attr: {\n        style: `\n          position: relative;\n          padding: 14px 16px;\n          background: var(--background-secondary);\n          border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);\n          border-top: 3px solid ${hub.color};\n          border-radius: 10px;\n          cursor: pointer;\n          display: flex;\n          flex-direction: column;\n          justify-content: space-between;\n          gap: 12px;\n          transition: transform 0.14s ease, border-color 0.14s ease, box-shadow 0.14s ease;\n        `\n      }\n    });\n\n    card.onmouseenter = () => {\n      card.style.transform = \"translateY(-2px)\";\n      card.style.borderColor = hub.color;\n      card.style.boxShadow = \"0 6px 16px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.22)\";\n    };\n    card.onmouseleave = () => {\n      card.style.transform = \"translateY(0)\";\n      card.style.borderColor = \"var(--background-modifier-border)\";\n      card.style.borderTop = `3px solid ${hub.color}`;\n      card.style.boxShadow = \"none\";\n    };\n\n    card.onclick = (e) => {\n      app.workspace.openLinkText(hub.file, dv.currentFilePath, e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey);\n    };\n\n    const topRow = card.createDiv({\n      attr: { style: \"display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; gap: 8px;\" }\n    });\n\n    topRow.createDiv({\n      text: hub.icon,\n      attr: {\n        style: `\n          font-size: 1.3rem;\n          width: 34px;\n          height: 34px;\n          display: flex;\n          align-items: center;\n          justify-content: center;\n          border-radius: 8px;\n          background: ${hub.color}18;\n          border: 1px solid ${hub.color}33;\n        `\n      }\n    });\n\n    topRow.createSpan({\n      text: hub.badge,\n      attr: {\n        style: `\n          font-size: 0.7rem;\n          font-weight: 600;\n          padding: 2px 8px;\n          border-radius: 12px;\n          background: ${hub.color}18;\n          color: ${hub.color};\n          border: 1px solid ${hub.color}33;\n          white-space: nowrap;\n        `\n      }\n    });\n\n    const bottomRow = card.createDiv({\n      attr: { style: \"display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;\" }\n    });\n\n    bottomRow.createDiv({\n      text: hub.title,\n      attr: { style: \"font-size: 1.05rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--text-normal);\" }\n    });\n\n    bottomRow.createSpan({\n      text: \"→\",\n      attr: { style: `font-size: 0.9rem; font-weight: 700; color: ${hub.color};` }\n    });\n  });\n}\n\n// ========================================================\n// 2. БЛОК «СЕГОДНЯ»: СОСТОЯНИЕ, ПОКАЗАТЕЛИ, ПЛАН И ИТОГИ ДНЯ\n// ========================================================\n{\n  const HABITS = [\n    { key: \"nofap\", name: \"NoFap\", color: \"#9c83e8\" },\n    { key: \"тренировка\", name: \"Тренировка\", color: \"#d69852\" },\n    { key: \"чтение\", name: \"Чтение\", color: \"#599ed6\" },\n    { key: \"режим_сна\", name: \"Режим сна\", color: \"#69b5aa\" },\n    { key: \"диета\", name: \"Диета\", color: \"#8bac5e\" },\n    { key: \"без_соцсетей\", name: \"Без соцсетей\", color: \"#e06c75\" },\n  ];\n\n  const COLORS = [\n    \"#d85b57\", \"#d97354\", \"#d98c54\", \"#d9a554\", \"#c5ad55\",\n    \"#a6b25a\", \"#8cb666\", \"#73b473\", \"#5ab17f\", \"#4aab80\"\n  ];\n\n  function computeSleepDurationHours(sleepTimeStr, wakeTimeStr) {\n    if (!sleepTimeStr || !wakeTimeStr) return null;\n    const sMatch = String(sleepTimeStr).trim().match(/^(\\d{1,2})[:.](\\d{2})$/);\n    const wMatch = String(wakeTimeStr).trim().match(/^(\\d{1,2})[:.](\\d{2})$/);\n    if (!sMatch || !wMatch) return null;\n    const sh = Number(sMatch[1]), sm = Number(sMatch[2]);\n    const wh = Number(wMatch[1]), wm = Number(wMatch[2]);\n    if (sh < 0 || sh > 23 || sm < 0 || sm > 59 || wh < 0 || wh > 23 || wm < 0 || wm > 59) return null;\n    let diff = (wh * 60 + wm) - (sh * 60 + sm);\n    if (diff <= 0) diff += 24 * 60;\n    return Math.round((diff / 60) * 10) / 10;\n  }\n\n  function normalizeTimeString(v) {\n    if (!v) return \"\";\n    const m = String(v).trim().match(/^(\\d{1,2})[:.](\\d{2})$/);\n    if (!m) return \"\";\n    const h = Number(m[1]), min = Number(m[2]);\n    if (h < 0 || h > 23 || min < 0 || min > 59) return \"\";\n    return `${String(h).padStart(2, \"0\")}:${String(min).padStart(2, \"0\")}`;\n  }\n\n  const INPUT_METRICS = [\n    { label: \"Вес\", key: \"вес_кг\", unit: \"кг\", min: 20, max: 300, digits: 1, placeholder: \"—\" },\n    { label: \"Сон\", key: \"сон_часов\", unit: \"ч\", min: 0, max: 24, digits: 1, placeholder: \"—\", hint: `авто-режим >${AUTO_SLEEP_MIN_HOURS} ч` },\n    { label: \"Чтение\", key: \"страниц_прочитано\", unit: \"стр.\", min: 0, max: 1500, digits: 0, placeholder: \"—\", hint: readingSync.autoBookPages > 0 ? `из книг: ${readingSync.autoBookPages} стр.` : \"авто из читалки\" },\n    { label: \"Соцсети\", key: \"соцсети_мин\", unit: \"мин\", min: 0, max: 1440, digits: 0, placeholder: \"—\", hint: `лимит ≤${SOCIAL_LIMIT_MIN} мин` },\n    { label: \"Шаги\", key: \"шаги\", unit: \"шаг\", min: 0, max: 100000, digits: 0, placeholder: \"—\", hint: \"активность за день\" },\n  ];\n\n  function formatNumForInput(v, digits = 1) {\n    const n = parseNum(v);\n    if (n === null) return \"\";\n    const rounded = Math.round(n * 10 ** digits) / 10 ** digits;\n    return String(Object.is(rounded, -0) ? 0 : rounded).replace(\".\", \",\");\n  }\n\n  function openNutritionFromHome() {\n    const plugin = getNutritionPlugin();\n    if (!plugin || typeof plugin.openNutrition !== \"function\") {\n      notify(\"Включи плагин «Питание и БЖУ» (my-nutrition-tracker) в настройках Obsidian.\");\n      return;\n    }\n    try {\n      plugin.openNutrition({ dateISO: todayISO });\n    } catch (err) {\n      notify(\"Не удалось открыть дневник питания: \" + (err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)));\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (!window.__dhTodayDrafts || window.__dhTodayDrafts.dateISO !== todayISO) {\n    window.__dhTodayDrafts = {\n      dateISO: todayISO,\n      inputs: {},\n      errors: {},\n      savedStamp: {},\n      activeKey: null,\n      selectionStart: null,\n      selectionEnd: null\n    };\n  }\n  const uiDrafts = window.__dhTodayDrafts;\n\n  if (!window.__dhTodaySaveQueue) {\n    window.__dhTodaySaveQueue = Promise.resolve();\n  }\n  function enqueueFrontMatterSave(file, mutator) {\n    if (!file) return Promise.reject(new Error(\"Файл ежедневной заметки не найден\"));\n    const nextTask = window.__dhTodaySaveQueue\n      .catch(() => {})\n      .then(() => app.fileManager.processFrontMatter(file, mutator));\n    window.__dhTodaySaveQueue = nextTask;\n    return nextTask;\n  }\n\n  const note = todayNote;\n  const trainings = trainingsTodayList;\n\n  const root = dv.container.createDiv({ cls: \"dh dh-today\" });\n\n  const styleEl = root.createEl(\"style\");\n  /* CSS перенесён в глобальный styles.css плагина */\n\n  function actionButton(parent, label, run, cls = \"dh-btn\") {\n    const b = parent.createEl(\"button\", { cls, text: label, attr: { type: \"button\" } });\n    b.onmousedown = e => e.preventDefault();\n    b.onclick = async () => {\n      if (b.disabled) return;\n      b.disabled = true;\n      try {\n        await run();\n      } catch (e) {\n        notify(`Не получилось: ${e?.message ?? e}`);\n      } finally {\n        if (b.isConnected) b.disabled = false;\n      }\n    };\n    return b;\n  }\n\n  const openToday = () => {\n    if (note) {\n      app.workspace.openLinkText(note.file.path, dv.currentFilePath, false);\n      return;\n    }\n    if (!app.commands.executeCommandById(\"daily-notes\")) {\n      notify(\"Включи основной плагин «Ежедневные заметки».\");\n    }\n  };\n\n  const head = root.createDiv({ cls: \"dh-today-head\" });\n  const titles = head.createDiv({ cls: \"dh-today-titles\" });\n  titles.createDiv({ cls: \"dh-kicker\", text: \"Сегодня\" });\n\n  const dateRow = titles.createDiv({ cls: \"dh-today-date-row\" });\n  dateRow.createDiv({ cls: \"dh-today-date\", text: today.setLocale(\"ru\").toFormat(\"cccc, d MMMM\") });\n\n  const badgesWrap = dateRow.createDiv({ cls: \"dh-today-badges\" });\n\n  const actions = head.createDiv({ cls: \"dh-actions\" });\n  actionButton(\n    actions,\n    note ? \"Открыть запись дня\" : \"Создать запись дня\",\n    openToday,\n    note ? \"dh-btn\" : \"dh-btn mod-cta\"\n  );\n\n  if (!note) {\n    root.createDiv({\n      cls: \"dh-muted\",\n      text: \"Записи за сегодня пока нет. Нажми «Создать запись дня», чтобы отмечать оценку, энергию, привычки, показатели и главное событие прямо с главной.\",\n      attr: { style: \"margin-top: 12px; font-size: 0.84rem;\" }\n    });\n  } else {\n    const file = app.vault.getAbstractFileByPath(note.file.path);\n    const fmCache = file ? (app.metadataCache.getFileCache(file)?.frontmatter || {}) : {};\n    const manualOverrides = getManualHabitOverrides();\n\n    const saveField = (key, value) => enqueueFrontMatterSave(file, fm => {\n      fm[key] = value;\n    });\n\n    const savedCalories = parseNum(note.калории_ккал);\n    const savedProtein = parseNum(note.белки_г);\n    const savedFat = parseNum(note.жиры_г);\n    const savedCarbs = parseNum(note.углеводы_г);\n    const savedSleep = parseNum(note.сон_часов);\n    const savedPages = parseNum(note.страниц_прочитано);\n    const savedFocus = parseNum(note.глубокая_работа_часов);\n    const savedSocialMin = parseNum(note.соцсети_мин);\n    const effectivePages = readingSync.autoBookPages > 0 && (savedPages === null || readingSync.totalPages > savedPages)\n      ? readingSync.totalPages\n      : savedPages;\n\n    const focusByGoalsMap = (fmCache.фокус_по_целям && typeof fmCache.фокус_по_целям === \"object\")\n      ? fmCache.фокус_по_целям\n      : {};\n\n    const states = new Map(HABITS.map(h => [h.key, typeof note[h.key] === \"boolean\" ? note[h.key] : null]));\n    const pendingPatch = {};\n\n    // 1. Автосинхронизация страниц чтения из книг и привычки «Чтение»\n    if (readingSync.autoBookPages > 0 && (savedPages === null || readingSync.totalPages > savedPages)) {\n      pendingPatch.страниц_прочитано = readingSync.totalPages;\n    }\n    if ((effectivePages !== null && effectivePages > 0) || readingSync.readBookTitles.length > 0) {\n      if (manualOverrides.чтение === undefined && states.get(\"чтение\") !== true) {\n        states.set(\"чтение\", true);\n        pendingPatch.чтение = true;\n      }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Автосинхронизация привычки «Тренировка» при наличии сессии за сегодня\n    if (trainings.length > 0 && manualOverrides.тренировка === undefined && states.get(\"тренировка\") !== true) {\n      states.set(\"тренировка\", true);\n      pendingPatch.тренировка = true;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Автосинхронизация привычки «Диета» только если в my-nutrition-tracker задана дневная цель калорий\n    if (calorieGoal !== null && savedCalories !== null && savedCalories >= calorieGoal && manualOverrides.диета === undefined && states.get(\"диета\") !== true) {\n      states.set(\"диета\", true);\n      pendingPatch.диета = true;\n    }\n\n    // 4. goalEntries.map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v} мин`).join(\", \") }\n      });\n\n      // Живое обновление карточки на главной, если сейчас запущен таймер или секундомер\n      if (window.__dhHomeFocusTicker) {\n        window.clearInterval(window.__dhHomeFocusTicker);\n        delete window.__dhHomeFocusTicker;\n      }\n\n      const paintRunningState = () => {\n        const curSession = getFocusPlugin()?.session;\n        if (!curSession || !curSession.isRunning) return;\n        if (curSession.endAt !== null) {\n          const remSec = Math.max(0, Math.ceil((curSession.endAt - Date.now()) / 1000));\n          const mm = String(Math.floor(remSec / 60)).padStart(2, \"0\");\n          const ss = String(remSec % 60).padStart(2, \"0\");\n          valSpan.setText(`${mm}:${ss}`);\n          unitSpan.setText(\"ост.\");\n        } else {\n          const elSec = Math.max(0, Math.floor((Date.now() - curSession.startAt) / 1000));\n          const mm = String(Math.floor(elSec / 60)).padStart(2, \"0\");\n          const ss = String(elSec % 60).padStart(2, \"0\");\n          valSpan.setText(`${mm}:${ss}`);\n          unitSpan.setText(\"сек.\");\n        }\n        statusBadgeEl.setText(\"🔥 Идёт\");\n        hintEl.setText(`Цель: ${curSession.project}`);\n      };\n\n      if (isRunningNow) {\n        paintRunningState();\n        window.__dhHomeFocusTicker = window.setInterval(() => {\n          if (!focusCard.isConnected) {\n            window.clearInterval(window.__dhHomeFocusTicker);\n            return;\n          }\n          paintRunningState();\n        }, 1000);\n      }\n\n      focusCard.onclick = () => openFocusTrackerFromHome();\n      focusCard.onkeydown = e => {\n        if (e.key === \"Enter\" || e.key === \" \") {\n          e.preventDefault();\n          openFocusTrackerFromHome();\n        }\n      };\n    }\n\n    renderTimeMetricCard(\"Отбой\", \"отбой\", \"23:30\", \"время засыпания\");\n    renderTimeMetricCard(\"Подъём\", \"подъём\", \"07:15\", \"время пробуждения\");\n    renderInputMetricCard(INPUT_METRICS[3]); // Соцсети (мин)\n    renderInputMetricCard(INPUT_METRICS[4]); // Шаги\n\n    const bottomGrid = body.createDiv({ cls: \"dh-today-bottom-grid\" });\n\n    if (goalsState.length > 0) {\n      const planBox = bottomGrid.createDiv({ cls: \"dh-plan-box\" });\n      const planHead = planBox.createDiv({ cls: \"dh-plan-head\" });\n      planHead.createSpan({ cls: \"dh-plan-title\", text: \"План дня (задачи из ежедневника)\" });\n      const planMeta = });\n\n      const planList = planBox.createDiv({ cls: \"dh-plan-list\" });\n      const itemPainters = [];\n\n      const refreshPlanUI = () => {\n        const { totalG, doneG, avgPct } =
